Fixes only the errors with minimal diffs, no refactors or redesign, and reruns the build until it is green or a guardrail stops it. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-build-error-resolver.md))
- **neva-code-architect**: Dispatch after exploration and before implementing a feature to get a blueprint that fits existing patterns: files to create and modify, interfaces, data flow, and build order. Read-only. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-code-architect.md))
- **neva-code-explorer**: Dispatch before changing an unfamiliar area to trace how an existing feature works: entry points, execution flow, layers, dependencies, and reusable pieces. Read-only, returns a written report. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-code-explorer.md))
- **neva-code-reviewer**: Dispatch after writing or modifying code, before commit or merge. Confidence-gated review of the diff for security, data safety, races, correctness, and quality. Returns severity-ranked findings with file:line and APPROVE, WARNING, or BLOCK. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-code-reviewer.md))
- **neva-code-simplifier**: Dispatch after a feature works to simplify recently changed code for clarity and consistency while preserving behavior exactly. Not for bug fixes or new features. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-code-simplifier.md))
- **neva-comment-analyzer**: Dispatch during PR review or after doc-heavy changes to check code comments against the code: accuracy, completeness, stale references, and rot risk. Advisory findings only. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-comment-analyzer.md))
- **neva-conversation-analyzer**: Dispatch from /hookify with no arguments, or when asked which behaviors to block, to mine the conversation for corrections, reverts, and repeated mistakes and propose hookify rules. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-conversation-analyzer.md))
- **neva-doc-updater**: Dispatch after features, API routes, dependencies, or setup change to regenerate codemaps and sync READMEs and guides from source of truth. Backs /update-docs and /update-codemaps. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-doc-updater.md))
- **neva-docs-lookup**: Dispatch when a question needs current library, framework, or API docs rather than training data. Fetches via Context7 and returns a short answer with examples and the source. ([Laith0003/neva](https://github.com/Laith0003/neva/blob/HEAD/adapters/cursor/.cursor/agents/neva-docs-lookup.md))
- **neva-e2e-runner**: Dispatch to write, run, or stabilize end-to-end tests for critical user journeys. - **neva-gan-evaluator**: Dispatched by /gan-build each iteration: tests the running app against the eval rubric, scores it strictly, and writes actionable feedback for the generator. - **neva-gan-generator**: Dispatched by /gan-build each iteration: implements or improves the app from gan-harness/spec.md and the latest evaluator feedback, keeps the dev server running, and commits. - **neva-gan-planner**: Dispatched by /gan-build at the start: expands a one-line brief into a product spec, sprint plan, and eval rubric under gan-harness/. - **neva-harness-optimizer**: Dispatch when agent harness config (hooks, agents, skills, commands, settings) needs tuning for reliability or cost. - **neva-loop-operator**: Dispatch to run or supervise an autonomous agent loop: checks gates, tracks checkpoints, detects stalls and retry storms, and pauses or escalates when progress stops. - **neva-opensource-sanitizer**: Dispatch as stage two of the opensource-pipeline skill and before any public release: independently audits a fork for leaked secrets, PII, and internal references. - **neva-performance-optimizer**: Dispatch when code is slow, memory grows, bundles are heavy, or renders lag. - **neva-planner**: Dispatch before implementing a multi-step feature, refactor, or architectural change. Read-only. - **neva-pr-test-analyzer**: Dispatch during PR review to judge whether the tests actually prevent regressions: behavioral coverage, missing edge and error cases, weak assertions, and over-mocking. - **neva-rag-pipeline-reviewer**: Dispatch when a RAG system, vector store, chunking, embedding, or retrieval evaluation is built, changed, or underperforming. - **neva-refactor-cleaner**: Dispatch to find and remove dead code, unused exports, unused dependencies, and duplicates. - **neva-silent-failure-hunter**: Dispatch during review of error-handling code or after an incident with no logs: finds swallowed errors, empty catches, bad fallbacks, and missing error propagation.
